### [1] [DeltaWAM: Delta World Action Models for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28811)
*Han Yan,Zishang Xiang,Haokai Jiang,Zeyu Zhang,Qilin Wang,Weiyu Guo,Yandong Guo,Boxin Shi,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeltaWAM 通过联合预测视觉增量与动作，并引入流式增量记忆，显著降低训练与推理开销，同时提升机器人操作任务的成功率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAMs）虽能从预训练视频生成器迁移视觉与运动先验到机器人控制，但存在两个核心问题：训练时预测稠密未来帧，重复建模大量几乎不变的内容，并将动作条件动态与无关外观变化耦合；推理时对完整观测使用重型视频专家，成为少步动作生成的瓶颈。因此，需要更稀疏地建模视觉动态、解耦外观干扰，并降低推理阶段视频专家计算量。

Method: 提出 DeltaWAM，联合预测视觉增量（visual deltas）与动作，采用 dense-anchor、sparse-delta 和 action 三条流，并设计三种在表示与计算共享方式上不同的架构。进一步提出 Streaming Delta Memory（SDM），利用紧凑的观测增量更新缓存的锚点上下文，以减少重型视频专家的重复处理。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 上，DeltaWAM+SDM 相比 Fast-WAM 将平均成功率从 81.3% 提升至 85.4%（干净设置），在视觉随机化下从 75.8% 提升至 83.9%。三种架构使训练 FLOPs 降低 17.78%–23.77%；SDM 将单步推理延迟和 FLOPs 分别降低 36.57% 和 31.55%。真实世界评估中，在对比策略里取得最高总体成功率和归一化进度。

Conclusion: DeltaWAM 通过增量式视觉建模与流式增量记忆，有效缓解了 WAM 中稠密帧预测和重型视频专家带来的效率瓶颈，并在仿真与真实机器人任务中同时提升了成功率、鲁棒性与计算效率。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) transfer visual and motion priors from pretrained video generators to robot control by jointly modeling visual dynamics and actions. Existing WAMs, however, predict dense future frames during training, repeatedly modeling largely unchanged content and coupling action-conditioned dynamics to nuisance appearance variations. At inference, processing each complete observation with the heavy video expert bottlenecks few-step action generation. Accordingly, we propose DeltaWAM, which jointly predicts visual deltas and actions using dense-anchor, sparse-delta, and action streams, with three architectures that differ in representation and computation sharing. We further develop Streaming Delta Memory (SDM), which updates cached anchor context with compact observed deltas, reducing heavy video-expert processing. On RoboTwin, DeltaWAM with SDM improves average success over Fast-WAM from 81.3% to 85.4% in the clean setting and from 75.8% to 83.9% under visual randomization. The three architectures reduce training FLOPs by 17.78-23.77%, while SDM reduces one-step inference latency and FLOPs by 36.57% and 31.55%, respectively; real-world evaluations further show the highest overall success rate and normalized progress among the evaluated policies. Code: https://github.com/AIGeeksGroup/DeltaWAM. Website: https://aigeeksgroup.github.io/DeltaWAM.

</details>


### [2] [Direction-Scale Decomposition in Action Representation: Rethinking What to Tokenize for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28865)
*Yufei Duan,Hang Yin,Alberta Longhini,Chao Tang,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出 Direction-Scale Decomposition (DSD) 动作表示：在 tokenization 前将平移和旋转增量分解为方向与尺度分量，用于离散 token VLA 学习。实验表明 DSD 可提升 LIBERO、SimplerEnv 及真机操作任务成功率，尤其在混合数据集训练下缓解性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作表示在离散 token 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）学习中至关重要，但研究不足。传统 pose-increment 表示对执行速度和数据集特定归一化敏感，可能掩盖跨演示、跨数据集的共享几何结构，从而限制模型泛化与大规模数据混合训练效果。

Method: 提出 DSD（Direction-Scale Decomposition）动作表示：将平移与旋转增量分解为 direction（方向）和 scale（尺度）分量后再进行 tokenization，使运动方向与幅值在独立通道中建模。作者将 DSD 与 uniform binning（BIN）及基于 B 样条的 BEAST tokenizer 结合，在仿真和真机操作任务中，分别评估单数据集与混合数据集训练设置。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上，DSD 结合两种 tokenizer 均提升平均成功率；在 SimplerEnv 混合数据集训练下，DSD-BIN 的总体成功率比 BIN 高 10.3 个百分点；真机实验显示，无论是否使用机器人预训练，DSD 均带来增益。

Conclusion: DSD 是一种有效的离散 token VLA 动作表示，能够提升操作任务成功率，并有望缓解在大型、多样化数据集混合训练时的性能退化问题。项目页面：https://vla-dsd.github.io/

Abstract: Action representation plays a central role in discrete-token vision-language-action (VLA) learning but remains underexamined. Under conventional pose-increment representations, action tokens are sensitive to execution speed and dataset-specific normalization, potentially obscuring geometric structure shared across demonstrations and datasets. We introduce Direction-Scale Decomposition (DSD), an action representation that decomposes translation and rotation increments into direction and scale components before tokenization. DSD isolates motion direction while retaining magnitudes in separate scale channels. We evaluate DSD with uniform binning (BIN) and BEAST, a B-spline-based tokenizer, in simulation and real-world manipulation under both single-dataset and mixed-dataset training. On LIBERO, DSD improves average success rates with both tokenizers. On SimplerEnv, DSD-BIN outperforms BIN by 10.3 percentage points in overall success rate under mixed-dataset training. Real-robot experiments further show gains both with and without robotics pretraining. These results support DSD as an effective action representation for discrete-token VLA models and suggest its potential to mitigate performance degradation when training on large and diverse dataset mixtures. Our project page with additional resources is available at https://vla-dsd.github.io/

</details>


### [3] [UpDown-SC: Gravity-Canonicalized Dual-Envelope Scan Context for Indoor LiDAR Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29118)
*Jie Xu,Yongxin Yang,Ziyi Jin,Kangjin Yu,Hongjun Huang,Chao Han,Zhongpu Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的极坐标描述子UpDown-SC，通过重力对齐与上下互补包络建模，缓解室内Scan Context因天花板抑制中低层几何导致的检索退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR地点识别是回环检测与全局重定位的前端，但室内场景在姿态或传感器安装高度变化时检索困难。Scan Context以极坐标单元最大高度编码，室内宽大天花板会压制区分相邻房间/走廊的中低层几何信息。

Method: UpDown-SC免训练：先做重力规范化，再表示两个互补表面——中低层结构的上包络与顶部结构的下包络；其物理分界由单元平衡的地图高度分布一次性估计并复用于所有查询。采用掩码感知、非均匀双通道距离，保留判别性低层证据、限制跨会话变化敏感性，且不将未观测单元当作零高度。保留Scan Context候选筛选与环形偏航对齐，检索假设可直接初始化几何验证。

Result: 在重复室内会话、安装高度变化、室内外混合轨迹及室外迁移序列上，室内和高度变化会话的首选检索更可靠；配对检验显示在自采室内会话上相对Scan Context显著提升。阈值接受下F1max与AUPR达到最佳或次佳，同时保持轻量CPU前端。连续回放验证检索假设可支持度量先验地图定位。

Conclusion: UpDown-SC通过上下包络与掩码感知双通道距离，在不训练、轻量CPU条件下提升室内及安装高度变化下的LiDAR地点识别鲁棒性，并可为后续几何验证与度量定位提供有效初值。代码与评测工件已开源。

Abstract: LiDAR place recognition is a key front end for loop closure and global relocalization, yet indoor retrieval remains difficult when attitude or sensor mounting height changes between mapping and query sessions. Scan Context stores the maximum height in each polar cell; indoors, broad ceilings can suppress the lower and mid-level geometry that distinguishes adjacent rooms and corridors. We present UpDown-SC, a training-free polar descriptor that first canonicalizes gravity and then represents two complementary surfaces: the upper envelope of lower/middle structures and the lower envelope of overhead structures. Their physical split is estimated once from a cell-balanced map height distribution and reused by every query. A mask-aware, non-uniform two-channel distance retains discriminative lower-level evidence while limiting sensitivity to its cross-session variation, without treating unobserved cells as zero-height measurements. Conventional Scan Context shortlisting and circular yaw alignment are retained, so retrieved hypotheses directly initialize geometric verification. Experiments across repeated indoor sessions, mounting-height changes, mixed outdoor-to-indoor trajectories, and an outdoor transfer sequence show more reliable first-choice retrieval on the indoor and mounting-height-varied sessions. A paired test finds a significant gain over Scan Context on the in-house sessions. UpDown-SC also gives the best or second-best F1max and AUPR under threshold-based acceptance while retaining a lightweight CPU front end. Continuous replay confirms that the retrieved hypotheses support metric prior-map localization. Code and evaluation artifacts: https://github.com/jiejie567/updown-sc.

</details>


### [4] [DrGait: Biomechanically Grounded Visual Reasoning for Interpretable Clinical Gait Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28796)
*Xiangyu Yin,Shiqi Wang,Abrar Alamri,Yasir Aljohani,Weichen Liu,Goeran Fiedler,Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DrGait：一种无需训练的智能体框架，将 VLM 从直接视觉推理者转变为临床规划者，通过 Triage-Verification-Synthesis 流程解耦语义推理与几何感知，减少步态分析中的幻觉并生成可审计临床报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床步态分析依赖不可解释的黑盒分类器；VLM 虽具推理能力，但直接处理步态视频时难以测量细微几何偏差，易产生幻觉。因此需要一种既能利用 VLM 语义推理、又能锚定可验证几何测量的方法。

Method: DrGait 是 training-free 的 agentic framework。输入视频和基础时空指标后，执行结构化 Triage-Verification-Synthesis (TVS) 工作流：先启发式 triage 生成诊断假设；再自主调用确定性生物力学工具进行验证，工具基于重建的 3D mesh 轨迹、分割的 2D pose 轨迹和事件中心视频证据；最后通过闭环机制根据反馈递归更新推理上下文。

Result: 通过将 VLM 推理锚定在可验证的几何与时间测量上，DrGait 降低了幻觉，取得有竞争力的诊断准确率，并生成透明、可审计的临床报告。

Conclusion: DrGait 表明，将 VLM 定位为临床规划者而非直接视觉推理者，并通过工具调用与闭环验证保障几何感知，可在无需训练的情况下提升临床步态分析的可解释性、可靠性和可审计性。

Abstract: Current automated gait analysis for clinical applications relies on uninterpretable black-box classifiers. Although Vision-Language Models (VLMs) offer strong reasoning capabilities, applying them directly to gait videos often leads to hallucinations, because they struggle to measure subtle geometric deviations from raw visual contexts. To address this, we introduce DrGait, a training-free agentic framework that shifts the VLM's role from a direct visual reasoner to a clinical planner. DrGait decouples semantic reasoning from geometric perception through a structured Triage-Verification-Synthesis (TVS) workflow. Given an input video and a set of basic spatiotemporal metrics, the DrGait agent first performs a heuristic triage to propose diagnostic hypotheses, which are then verified by autonomously calling deterministic biomechanical tools that operate on reconstructed 3D mesh trajectories, segmented 2D pose tracks, and event-centered video evidence. Finally, a closed-loop mechanism recursively updates the agent's reasoning context based on the feedback. By anchoring VLM's reasoning in verifiable geometric and temporal measurements, DrGait reduces hallucinations, achieving competitive diagnostic accuracy while generating transparent and audit-ready clinical reports.

</details>


### [5] [M$^2$PFN: End-to-End Disentangled Alignment for Generalizable Multimodal In-Context Learning in Alzheimer's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28836)
*Lujia Zhong,Shuo Huang,Jianwei Zhang,Xinyu Nie,Yonggang Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: M²PFN将表格基础模型TabPFN的上下文学习扩展到多模态阿尔茨海默病诊断，通过端到端可微推理、跨模态对齐和门控捷径，在ADNI及外部队列上取得了优于基线的分类与MMSE回归性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态AD诊断方法结合影像与表格数据，但跨队列泛化能力有限。TabPFN等表格基础模型具有强泛化性和灵活性，但其元训练依赖合成表格先验，与影像派生特征的统计结构不匹配，因此难以直接扩展到多模态AD分析。

Method: 提出M²PFN端到端框架，将TabPFN转化为多模态AD预测器。具体包括：(i) 通过TabPFN的Transformer进行可微推理，将任务梯度反传至3D-MRI和表格编码器；(ii) 利用解耦与对比目标，将两种模态对齐到与ICL引擎先验匹配的共享子空间；(iii) 通过可学习门控捷径融合冻结的纯表格预测。ICL引擎保持冻结以保留测试时泛化的上下文机制，端到端训练则用于塑造编码器，使其生成可被TabPFN利用的特征。

Result: 在ADNI队列（n=2240，三分类CN/MCI/AD）上，M²PFN达到65.55% macro-F1和82.21% macro-AUC，超过多种单模态和多模态基线。仅替换为TabPFN回归头后，同一架构在1250名受试者子队列上回归基线MMSE，测试MAE为1.743，优于所有多模态基线。在两个外部队列OASIS-3和SCAN上无需重训练，M²PFN取得所有基线中最佳AUC和最低MMSE MAE，并且在认知量表变化时仍能迁移。

Conclusion: M²PFN有效弥合了ICL表格基础模型与多模态医学数据之间的先验差距，提升了AD诊断分类和认知评分回归的跨队列泛化能力。

Abstract: While various multimodal methods combining imaging and tabular data for Alzheimer's disease (AD) diagnosis were proposed, they are often limited in generalization across cohorts. In-context learning (ICL) has demonstrated excellent generalization performances and high flexibility in foundational tabular models such as TabPFN. To extend TabPFN's ICL to multimodal AD analysis, the main obstacle is that TabPFN is meta-trained on synthetic tabular priors that do not naturally match the statistical structure of image-derived features. We propose M$^2$PFN, an end-to-end framework that turns this tabular foundation model into a multimodal AD predictor. M$^2$PFN (i) performs differentiable inference through TabPFN's transformer, back-propagating task gradients into 3D-MRI and tabular encoders; (ii) aligns the two modalities into a shared subspace, via disentanglement and a contrastive objective, matched to the ICL engine's prior; and (iii) folds in a frozen tabular-only prediction through a learnable gated shortcut. Because the ICL engine stays frozen, its in-context mechanism is preserved for test-time generalization, while end-to-end training shapes the encoders into features it can exploit. On ADNI ($n=2240$, three-class CN/MCI/AD), M$^2$PFN attains $65.55\%$ macro-F1 and $82.21\%$ macro-AUC, surpassing a comprehensive set of unimodal and multimodal baselines. By swapping only the head for a TabPFN regressor, the same architecture regresses baseline MMSE on a $1250$-subject sub-cohort to test MAE $1.743$, outperforming every multimodal baseline. On two external cohorts (OASIS-3 and SCAN) with no retraining, M$^2$PFN achieves the best AUC and the lowest MMSE MAE across all baselines, and transfers even when the cognitive instrument changes.

</details>


### [6] [UltraBench 2: Towards Robust Evaluation of Vision Foundation Models on Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28610)
*Ashwath Radhachandran,Adam Tupper,Christian Gagné,William Speier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UltraBench 2 超声影像基础模型综合基准，覆盖多解剖部位与多任务，强调标准化、可复现和易用性，并发现超声专用预训练在分类上仍领先，而先进通用模型在分割上已追平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声基础模型近年快速发展，但评测基准建设滞后，导致不同模型评估碎片化、不一致，难以衡量领域真实进展。

Method: 构建 UltraBench 2：一个具有广泛解剖覆盖和任务覆盖的综合基准，注重标准化、可复现性与易用性；并利用该基准对现有视觉基础模型在超声图像分析中的表现进行对比评估。

Result: 超声专用预训练在分类任务上仍然领先；最先进的通用视觉基础模型在分割任务上已与超声专用模型持平。

Conclusion: UltraBench 2 为超声基础模型提供了更统一、可复现的评测框架，揭示不同预训练范式在分类与分割上的优势差异，有助于更可靠地衡量该领域进展。

Abstract: Benchmarking is an increasingly critical part of research in machine learning and the domains where it is applied, including healthcare. Yet, despite the steady development of new ultrasound foundation models in recent years, the development of well-designed benchmarks to evaluate them has lagged behind. This deficiency has led to fragmented and inconsistent evaluations of competing models, making it difficult to measure progress. To address this issue, we introduce UltraBench 2, a comprehensive benchmark with wide anatomical and task coverage, and a focus on standardization, reproducibility, and ease-of-use. Using this benchmark, we compare existing vision foundation models for ultrasound image analysis. Our analyses demonstrate that ultrasound-specific pretraining still leads on classification, but that state-of-the-art general-purpose models have drawn level on segmentation.

</details>


### [7] [Beneath the Scores: Rethinking Hallucination Evaluation for Video Understanding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28991)
*Shuzhi Gong,Fengze Sun,Yuansan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出因果阶段干预协议，系统定位多阶段视频LLM智能体的幻觉来源，发现时序定位（grounding）是下游错误的主导因素，现有基准分数无法可靠预测错误级联与分布偏移下的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频理解日益依赖多阶段LLM智能体，将时序定位、视觉观察与推理分离；但这些阶段通常在不同基准和分布上评测，导致难以确定幻觉究竟源自哪个阶段。现有阶段级分数提供的诊断信号不一致：更强的阶段性能并不一定带来更低的下游幻觉，即使针对同一能力的基准也可能相互矛盾。

Method: 作者围绕上述阶段组织现有基准，并提出一种因果阶段干预协议：在保持下游任务固定的前提下，分别覆盖/干预单个阶段。该协议在三种视频智能体架构上进行了60,008次运行，以量化各阶段对下游错误的因果影响，并审计现有基准能否预测错误级联与分布偏移表现。

Result: 结果表明，grounding是下游错误的主要来源，其因果影响约为破坏视觉观察的4倍。成功grounding主要取决于定位到正确区域，而非精确的时序重叠，这解释了标准mIoU指标为何难以预测下游可靠性。错误证据比缺失证据危害更大。对现有基准的审计还发现，其分数不能可靠预测因果级联敏感性，并可能在分布偏移下失效。

Conclusion: 论文主张面向可信视频智能体，应采用基于干预、阶段感知的评测范式，而非依赖传统阶段级基准分数，以更可靠地诊断和预测下游幻觉与错误传播。

Abstract: Video understanding is increasingly performed by multi-stage LLM agents that separate temporal grounding, visual observation, and reasoning. Yet these stages are typically evaluated on different benchmarks and distributions, making it difficult to determine where hallucinations originate. We first organize existing benchmarks around these stages and show that their scores provide inconsistent diagnostic signals: stronger stage-level performance does not reliably imply lower downstream hallucination, and even benchmarks targeting the same capability can disagree.
  We therefore introduce a causal stage-intervention protocol that overwrites individual stages while holding the downstream task fixed. Across 60,008 runs on three video-agent architectures, we find that grounding is the dominant source of downstream error, with roughly four times the causal impact of corrupting visual observations. Successful grounding depends primarily on locating the correct region rather than precise temporal overlap, explaining why standard mIoU metrics poorly predict downstream reliability. We further find that incorrect evidence is substantially more harmful than missing evidence. Finally, auditing existing benchmarks against these interventions reveals that their scores do not reliably predict causal cascade sensitivity and can fail under distribution shift. These results motivate intervention-based, stage-aware evaluation for trustworthy video agents.

</details>


### [8] [M-plicits: Neural Implicit Surfaces via Nested Multiscale Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28684)
*Vinícius da Silva,Isabelle Melo,Matheus Bessa,Guilherme Schardong,Luiz Schirmer,André Araújo,Nuno Gonçalves,Hélio Lopes,Alberto Raposo,Luiz Velho,Tiago Novello*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: M-plicits 是一个多尺度隐式神经曲面表示框架，将曲面建模为一系列 MLP 的残差和，并通过嵌套邻域把监督严格限制在前一层零水平集附近的窄带内，从而实现高效训练、实时渲染与噪声鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式神经表示（INR）在训练效率、渲染速度与噪声鲁棒性之间难以兼顾：单 MLP 方法推理代价高；基于网格的表示虽快，但会限制曲面平滑性并对输入噪声过拟合；以往多尺度方法因硬性频谱截断而容易捕获噪声、产生伪影。因此需要一种能同时平衡三者的表示方法。

Method: 提出 M-plicits：1）将曲面表示为多尺度 MLP 的残差和，通过一系列嵌套邻域逐级训练；2）不同于依赖全域采样的残差方法，该方法将监督严格局部化在前一层零水平集周围的窄带内，无需昂贵的网格提取即可可视化；3）嵌套设计使粗网络充当低通滤波器建立干净几何先验，后续残差逐步细化而不拟合高频伪影；4）引入多尺度球体追踪算法与基于 GEMM 的解析法线计算，完全绕过自动微分。

Result: 在 Stanford 与 Thingi32 数据集上，M-plicits 在粗配置下取得最佳平均 Chamfer 距离，在精细配置下取得最佳中位 Chamfer 距离与 IoU；相比 iNGP、BACON、IDF 具有显著更好的噪声鲁棒性，且参数量比基于网格的基线少一个数量级。同时支持高保真实时渲染。

Conclusion: M-plicits 通过嵌套多尺度残差 MLP 与窄带局部监督，兼顾了训练效率、渲染速度与噪声鲁棒性，在保持曲面平滑性的同时以更少参数取得领先精度，并实现了实时高保真渲染；代码、模型与数据将开源。

Abstract: Encoding input coordinates with sinusoidal functions into multi-layer perceptrons (MLPs) has proven effective for implicit neural representations (INRs) of surfaces defined as zero-level sets. However, existing methods often struggle to balance training efficiency, rendering speed, and noise robustness: single-MLP approaches are expensive at inference, grid-based representations are fast but can limit surface smoothness and overfit input noise, and previous multiscale approaches frequently capture noise and produce artifacts due to hard spectral truncation. To address these limitations, we propose M-plicits, a multiscale framework that models surfaces as a residual sum of MLPs trained via a sequence of nested neighborhoods. Unlike existing residual approaches that rely on standard domain-wide sampling and require costly mesh extraction for visualization, our method strictly localizes supervision to narrow bands around the previous zero-level sets. This nested design naturally provides robustness against noisy input data: the coarse network acts as a low-pass filter that establishes a clean geometric prior, while subsequent residuals progressively refine the geometry without fitting to high-frequency artifacts. We further introduce a multiscale sphere-tracing algorithm and a GEMM-based analytical normal computation that bypasses auto-differentiation entirely, yielding high-fidelity real-time rendering. On Stanford and Thingi32, M-plicits achieves the best mean Chamfer distance in the coarse configuration and the best median Chamfer distance and IoU in the fine configuration, with substantially better noise robustness than iNGP, BACON, and IDF, while using an order of magnitude fewer parameters than grid-based baselines. Code, models, and data will be released at https://github.com/dsilvavinicius/m-plicits.

</details>


### [9] [EIB-Net: Entropy-Guided Information Bottleneck for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29064)
*Zhida Zhang,Xinlei Ma,Jie Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EIB-Net，利用图像熵引导选择低熵图块并结合变分信息瓶颈，实现跨生成模型、数据高效的AI生成图像检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成图像日益逼真，现有检测方法多针对局部伪造痕迹，而扩散模型等生成式图像缺乏此类痕迹，导致跨模型泛化困难。作者观察到生成模型重全局语义、轻局部纹理保真，低纹理区域可作为合成来源的关键线索。

Method: EIB-Net（Entropy-guided Information Bottleneck Network）：提出图像熵（IE）指标自动选取信息量最大（熵最低）的图块；再用变分信息瓶颈（VIB）处理该图块，学习紧凑且可泛化的特征；其中熵引导图块选择（EGPL）可迁移至CNN和Transformer等不同骨干。

Result: 在DIFF、DiffusionForensics、GenImage基准上达到SOTA：仅用2%训练数据取得85.7%准确率，较全图基线提升超15%；在GenImage上跨生成器平均准确率83.5%；EGPL能持续增强多种CNN与Transformer骨干。

Conclusion: 低熵图块是检测生成式图像的有效线索；熵引导+信息瓶颈可在少量数据下实现强泛化，并为数据高效检测提供通用模块。

Abstract: The proliferation of photorealistic AI-generated images demands robust detection methods that generalize across diverse generative models. While existing approaches target manipulation-based forgeries with local artifacts, generation-based images (e.g., from diffusion models) lack such traces, posing a fundamental challenge. We observe that generative models prioritize global semantics at the expense of local texture fidelity, making low-texture regions key indicators of synthetic origin. To exploit this, we propose EIB-Net, an Entropy-guided Information Bottleneck Network. EIB-Net introduces a novel Image Entropy (IE) metric to automatically select the most informative (lowest-entropy) patch, then processes it with a Variational Information Bottleneck (VIB) to learn compact, generalizable features. Extensive experiments on DIFF, DiffusionForensics, and GenImage benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance: EIB-Net achieves 85.7\% accuracy using only 2\% of training data, outperforming full-image baselines by over 15\%, and maintains robust cross-generator generalization (83.5\% average accuracy on GenImage). Furthermore, our entropy-guided patch selection (EGPL) consistently enhances diverse backbones (CNNs and Transformers), proving its practical value for data-efficient detection.

</details>


### [10] [WildHSR: Metric Feed-Forward 4D People-Scene Reconstruction from a 3D Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29106)
*Jerrin Bright,John Zelek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WildHSR 通过轻量适配一个 up-to-scale 3D 基础模型，同时读出度量尺度和人物身份，实现单目视频中联合的人-场景重建，并在 EMDB-2/RICH 上取得领先的前馈精度和 10.1 fps 速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D 基础模型虽可单次前向恢复相机与几何，但常仅达 up-to-scale；联合 people-scene reconstruction 还缺少 metric scale 与 persistent person identity。精确度量标签稀缺，而无标注野外视频丰富，因此研究一个 up-to-scale 表示能否经轻量适配同时支持二者。

Method: 方法包含两个读出模块：1）Scale Readout：利用 curated web video 中有姿态的度量人体与 2D 关键点生成闭式近似尺度伪标签，预训练后再用标准真实视频训练集精确度量监督微调，并与轻量 adapter 联合优化；推理时直接由基础模型 token 预测度量尺度。2）Identity：探查预训练基础模型，发现中间 query-key 特征编码跨帧人物对应，mid-layer token 在多数运动人物片段偏好该人物而非空出位置或他人；用极小投影读取对应关系，并结合 metric pelvis motion 与 proposal confidence，驱动 dustbin-aware Sinkhorn 关联逐帧人体。WildHSR 组合两读出，逐窗口前馈预测，通过解析关联与 Sim(3) composition 连接窗口。

Result: 在 EMDB-2 上，WildHSR 是已发表比较中首个在 WA-MPJPE 和 RTE 上超过最佳优化方法的前馈方法，并在三个 world-frame 指标上领先前馈方法；在 RICH 上，其在 WA-MPJPE 和 W-MPJPE 上领先前馈 people-and-scene 方法；完整流程单 GPU 达 10.1 fps。

Conclusion: 结果表明，up-to-scale 3D 基础表示经轻量适配即可同时提供度量尺度与持久人物身份，实现高效、前馈的单目联合人-场景重建。

Abstract: 3D foundation models recover video cameras and geometry in one forward pass, but some of the strongest are up to scale. Joint people-scene reconstruction then requires two missing outputs: metric scale and persistent person identity. We ask whether one up-to-scale foundation representation can support both through lightweight adaptation. Exact metric labels are scarce, but unlabeled in-the-wild video is abundant. We use people in curated web video to initialise the solution: a posed metric body and 2D keypoints give an approximate, closed-form scale pseudo-label. These pseudo-labels pretrain a Scale Readout, which is then fine-tuned together with a lightweight adapter using exact metric supervision from standard real-video training splits. At inference the head predicts metric scale from foundation-model tokens, without the ruler or its teachers. For person identity, we probe the pretrained foundation model alone and find evidence that its intermediate query-key features encode person correspondence across frames. In most evaluated moving-person clips, a mid-layer token prefers that person over the vacated location and other people. A tiny projection reads this correspondence; together with metric pelvis motion and proposal confidence, it drives dustbin-aware Sinkhorn association of per-frame bodies. WildHSR combines both readouts to reconstruct metric cameras, scene and people from monocular video. Each window is predicted feed-forward; analytic association and Sim(3) composition connect windows. On EMDB-2, WildHSR is the first feed-forward method in the published comparison to beat the best optimization-based WA-MPJPE and RTE while leading feed-forward methods on all three world-frame metrics. On RICH, it leads feed-forward people-and-scene methods on WA-MPJPE and W-MPJPE. The complete pipeline runs at 10.1 fps on one GPU.

</details>


### [11] [Multimodal Routing and Region Refinement for Language-Guided Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28860)
*Md Maklachur Rahman,Md Hasan Al Banna,Saraf Anjum,Assame Arnob,Tracy Hammond*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MRSeg 是一个参数高效的文本引导医学图像分割框架：冻结视觉与文本编码器，通过图像-文本对动态路由低秩适配器，并利用 Region Bridge 进行区域级跨模态细化，在多尺度解码下取得领先分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本描述可明确待分割的病灶类型与位置，从而降低医学图像分割的歧义。然而现有文本引导方法主要改进图文特征交互方式，通常对所有图文对采用同一条学习到的更新路径，难以针对不同样本自适应调整视觉与文本特征，限制了跨模态适配的灵活性与参数效率。

Method: 提出 MRSeg 参数高效框架。冻结 ConvNeXt-Tiny 与 PubMedBERT 编码器，分别提取多尺度视觉特征和临床文本 token。联合路由器利用最深视觉特征与池化文本预测低秩适配器基上的稀疏混合，并将该路由共享至两个视觉尺度与文本的独立适配器库，实现协同适配且保持特征特定参数分离。Region Bridge 以文本派生查询聚合稠密视觉 token 为潜在区域，经自注意力和文本交叉注意力细化后，将信息重分配到特征图。最后多尺度解码器融合细化语义特征与浅层图像证据。

Result: 在 QaTa-COV19 和 MosMedData+ 上分别达到 90.90/83.32 与 81.53/68.82 的 Dice/mIoU，可训练参数仅 7.11M，计算量为 7.60 GFLOPs。代码已开源。

Conclusion: MRSeg 通过样本级路由低秩适配器与区域桥接细化，在保持极低可训练参数和计算开销的同时，实现了文本引导医学图像分割的性能提升，验证了参数高效动态适配策略的有效性。

Abstract: Textual descriptions can reduce ambiguity in medical image segmentation by specifying the finding and location to be delineated. Existing text-guided methods mainly improve where image and language features interact but generally retain a single learned update pathway across all image-text pairs. We propose MRSeg, a parameter-efficient framework that uses each image-text pair to route the adaptation of visual and textual features before dense prediction. Frozen ConvNeXt-Tiny and PubMedBERT encoders provide multiscale visual features and clinical text tokens. A joint router uses the deepest visual feature and pooled text to predict a sparse mixture over low-rank adapter bases. The resulting route is shared across separate adapter banks for two visual scales and text, coordinating their adaptation while keeping the feature-specific parameters separate. Region Bridge uses text-derived queries to aggregate dense visual tokens into latent regions, refines these regions through self-attention and text cross-attention, and redistributes the refined information back to the feature maps. Finally, a multiscale decoder combines refined semantic features with shallow image evidence. On QaTa-COV19 and MosMedData+, MRSeg achieves 90.90/83.32 and 81.53/68.82 Dice/mIoU, respectively, with 7.11M trainable parameters and 7.60 GFLOPs. Code: https://github.com/maklachur/MRSeg.

</details>


### [12] [Less is More: Encoder-only Audio-Visual Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29121)
*Ilpo Viertola,Vladimir Iashin,Sophie Tötterström,Esa Rahtu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出仅编码器架构 EASE，用于音视频语义分割（AVSS），在精度相当下比此前 SotA 快 3 倍、最高 365 FPS，训练不足 11 GPU 小时，并在多种骨干和输入分辨率下取得 SotA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 Transformer 的 AVSS 方法大多沿用图像分割模型的设计，而近期研究表明这类图像分割模型包含对分割性能贡献很小的冗余组件，导致模型复杂、计算开销大，不利于实时应用与规模化研究。

Method: 提出 Encoder-only Audio-Visual Segmentation（EASE）。该方法遵循去除冗余组件的思路，采用仅编码器结构完成音视频语义分割，旨在简化 AVSS 架构并提升推理与训练效率；代码、模型权重和样例已开源。

Result: EASE 推理速度最高达 365 FPS，在精度相当的情况下比此前 SotA AVS 模型快 3 倍，训练时间少于 11 GPU 小时；同时在不同骨干网络和输入分辨率下均取得 SotA 的 AVSS 性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明 AVSS 可以更简单、更快速，为未来研究和实时应用提供了可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Semantic Segmentation (AVSS) aims to identify, segment, and classify sound-emitting objects in video frames. Previous Transformer-based AVSS approaches largely inherit design principles from image segmentation models. Recent studies show that these image segmentation models contain redundant components that contribute little to the segmentation performance. Following this insight, we propose Encoder-only Audio-Visual Segmentation (EASE). EASE runs at up to 365 FPS, 3x faster than prior State-of-the-Art (SotA) AVS models at comparable accuracy, and trains in under 11 GPU-hours. Furthermore, we achieve SotA AVSS performance across different backbones and input resolutions. Our results demonstrate that AVSS can be both simpler and faster, providing a scalable foundation for future research and real-time applications. Code, model weights, and samples are available at https://ease-avs.notion.site

</details>


### [13] [Med-AR: Autoregressive Vision-Language Pretraining for Long-Tailed Chest X-Ray Classification and Uncertainty-Aware Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29156)
*Janhavi Prabhu,Sahil,Akshay V,Shivam Shukla,Manoj Tadepalli,Preetham Putha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出放射学原生自回归视觉-语言模型 Med-AR-8B 与 Med-AR-2B，并系统评估其视觉编码器在长尾胸部X线多标签分类中的迁移能力。公开基准上，Med-AR 在 head/medium/tail 发现上的平均 AUROC 与 AUPRC 多优于对比方法，同时选择性预测表现更佳；内部结果则具有指标依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾胸部X线分类既需识别常见异常，也需捕捉细微、罕见发现。现有对比学习、自监督和监督预训练编码器在稀有尾部类别上的表征与迁移能力可能不足，亟需评估放射学原生自回归视觉-语言预训练的表征价值，并同时考察判别性能与选择性预测。

Method: 提出 Med-AR-8B 和 Med-AR-2B 两个放射学原生自回归视觉-语言模型，使用结构化报告、异常聚焦文本和区域标注进行预训练。将其视觉编码器迁移至多标签分类任务，并与 Med-CLIP、CheXFound、EVA-Base、ARK、BioViL-T 等对比、自监督和监督预训练编码器比较，统一使用 ML-Decoder 分类头。为评估细粒度识别，基于 LLM 扩展报告派生标签集，在 MIMIC-CXR 和 CheXpert 上构建标签；在 PadChest、MIMIC-CXR、CheXpert 上评估 head、medium、tail 分组的平均 AUROC/AUPRC，以及风险-覆盖率曲线下超额面积（excess AURC）衡量选择性预测。

Result: 在 PadChest、MIMIC-CXR、CheXpert 上，Med-AR-8B 在 head、medium、tail 发现上的平均 AUROC 和 AUPRC 优于 Med-CLIP；在 MIMIC-CXR 上将尾部标签平均 AUPRC 从 0.1033 提升至 0.1441。Med-AR-2B 在 PadChest 上取得最强判别结果。更广泛的编码器比较中，Med-AR 变体在每个公开数据集的每个患病率组别均取得最高平均 AUROC 和 AUPRC。两种 Med-AR 变体在三个公开数据集上的 excess AURC 均低于 Med-CLIP，表明选择性预测性能改善。内部结果依赖指标：Med-CLIP 在总体与尾部 AUPRC 及选择性预测上仍具优势。

Conclusion: Med-AR 是在所评估公开基准上用于长尾胸部X线分类的有力预训练方案，并表明应同时评估判别性能与选择性预测。内部结果提示优势并非在所有指标上一致，需结合具体评价指标与数据分布解释。

Abstract: Long-tailed chest X-ray classification requires visual representations that capture both common abnormalities and subtle, infrequent findings. We propose Med-AR-8B and Med-AR-2B, two radiology-native autoregressive vision-language models pretrained with structured reports, abnormality-focused text, and region annotations. We evaluate the transfer of their visual encoders to multi-label classification against contrastive, self-supervised, and supervised pretrained encoders, including Med-CLIP, CheXFound, EVA-Base, ARK, and BioViL-T, using a common ML-Decoder classification head. To assess fine-grained recognition, we also construct LLM-expanded, report-derived label sets for MIMIC-CXR and CheXpert. Across PadChest, MIMIC-CXR, and CheXpert, Med-AR-8B outperforms Med-CLIP in mean AUROC and AUPRC for head, medium, and tail findings. On MIMIC-CXR, it increases tail-label mean AUPRC from 0.1033 to 0.1441. Med-AR-2B achieves the strongest discrimination results on PadChest. Across the broader encoder comparison, a Med-AR variant achieves the highest mean AUROC and AUPRC in every reported prevalence group on each public dataset. Both Med-AR variants also achieve lower excess area under the risk-coverage curve than Med-CLIP on all three public datasets, indicating improved selective-prediction performance under the evaluated protocol. Internal results are metric-dependent, with Med-CLIP retaining advantages in overall and tail AUPRC and in selective prediction. These findings establish Med-AR as a strong pretraining recipe for long-tailed chest X-ray classification on the evaluated public benchmarks and demonstrate the value of assessing discrimination and selective prediction together.

</details>


### [14] [Exploiting answer-invariant redundancies in satellite imagery for efficient VLM inference on edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29029)
*Ishani Janveja,Davis Zhang,Seoyul Oh,Deepak Vasisht*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对星上视觉语言模型在高分辨率图像上逐块推理开销大的问题，提出“答案不变 token 冗余”（AITR）概念，并设计两阶段系统 Rift，通过查询条件化瓦片剪枝与弹性预填充降低 token 预算，在 Jetson AGX Orin 上实现能耗、延迟显著下降且精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星上视觉语言模型可直接响应查询，但对高分辨率遥感图像进行穷举式分块推理速度慢、能耗高，难以满足在轨部署需求。作者发现存在大量对最终答案无影响的图像瓦片与视觉 token，即 AITR，因此希望利用该冗余性降低计算与能耗。

Method: 提出 Rift 两阶段系统：第一阶段进行查询条件化的瓦片剪枝，依据查询动态剔除与答案无关的图像瓦片；第二阶段采用弹性预填充（elastic prefill）进一步压缩视觉 token 预算，从而减少推理所需 token 数量。

Result: 在 Jetson AGX Orin 上运行 LLaVA-1.5 7B 进行评估。与穷举式分块推理相比，Rift 将能耗降低 78%、延迟降低 69%，同时将准确率从 45% 提升至 73%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，显式建模并利用答案不变的 token 冗余，可在星上高分辨率视觉语言推理中同时实现能效、实时性与精度收益，为资源受限平台上的高效多模态推理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Onboard vision-language models could enable satellites to answer queries directly, but exhaustive tiled inference over high-resolution imagery is slow and energy-intensive. We identify answer-invariant token redundancy (AITR): image tiles and vision tokens that can be removed without changing the final answer. We present Rift, a two-stage system that performs query-conditioned tile pruning followed by elastic prefill to reduce token budget. We evaluate it on LLaVA-1.5 7B running on Jetson AGX Orin. Compared with exhaustive tiled inference, Rift reduces energy by 78% and latency by 69%, while increasing accuracy from 45% to 73%.

</details>


### [15] [Where Hallucinations Live: A Cross-Architecture Circuit in VQ-Tokenized Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29048)
*Shamanthak Hegde,Xiangrui Liu,Maitreya Patel,Yezhou Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过激活修补定位到统一VQ视觉语言模型中一个跨模型的早期层（L0）注意力路由电路，将其确立为对象幻觉的架构与预训练根源，并提出仅消融L0才能有效缓解开放生成中的幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一VLM通过向量量化（VQ）码本对图像分词，常在grounded yes/no基准上产生对象幻觉；现有解码期修正方法仅将其视为通用的校准偏差，缺乏架构层面的解释。作者希望从机制角度定位该病理信号的来源，并给出有针对性的干预方案。

Method: 作者在跨越8个LLM家族、共25个模型上使用激活修补（activation patching），识别出一个跨VQ-VLM共享的早期层（L0）注意力路由电路，并提出三闸门（three-gate）诊断以区分携带该电路的模型与不携带者。诊断筛出10个阳性模型（3个LLM家族下的5个自然统一VQ模型及5个诱导变体），排除其余15个。随后用单变量架构替换（LLaVA-1.6 CLIP+MLP → VQ+Linear）验证电路的安装，并设置等算力的MLP对照。最后与调优后的VCD、DoLA基线对比。

Result: 三闸门诊断隔离出10个阳性、拒绝15个模型；单变量VQ替换成功安装电路，而等算力MLP对照不能，证明向量量化是病理信号的来源，且承载该信号的路径由骨干网络本身提供。对比中，调优DoLA在二分类校准上胜出，但仅L0消融能降低开放生成的对象幻觉（CHAIR_i相对下降31%），而调优DoLA与VCD使其不变或恶化。

Conclusion: 该研究将统一VQ-VLM中的对象幻觉重新界定为架构与预训练的属性，而非通用校准问题，并给出了一种机制无关的解码方法无法复现的针对性干预（L0消融）。

Abstract: Unified vision-language models (VLMs) that tokenize images through a vector-quantized (VQ) codebook routinely hallucinate objects on grounded yes/no benchmarks, yet existing decoding-time fixes treat this as generic miscalibration without an architectural account. Using activation patching across twenty-five models spanning eight LLM families, we identify an early-layer ($L_0$) attention routing circuit shared across VQ-tokenized VLMs and propose a three-gate diagnostic that distinguishes the models carrying it from those that do not. The diagnostic isolates ten positive models (five natural unified-VQ VLMs across three LLM families and five induced variants) and rejects the remaining fifteen. A single-variable architectural swap (LLaVA-1.6 CLIP+MLP $\rightarrow$ VQ+Linear) installs the circuit, while a matched-compute MLP control on identical data does not, isolating vector quantization as the source of the pathological signal; the routing pathway that carries it is one that the backbone already provides. Against tuned VCD and DoLA baselines, tuned DoLA wins on binary calibration, but \textbf{only $L_0$ ablation reduces object hallucination in open-ended generation} (CHAIR$_i$ reduces by $31\,\%$ relatively, whereas tuned DoLA and VCD leave it unchanged or worsen it). These results recast object hallucination in unified VQ VLMs as a property of architecture and pretraining, and yield a targeted intervention that mechanism-agnostic decoding cannot replicate.

</details>


### [16] [SARFusion: Scene-Aware Routing Fusion for Robust Camera-LiDAR 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29235)
*Yuting Zhao,Ziyi Zheng,Shuxiao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SARFusion：将鲁棒相机-激光雷达融合建模为场景感知分支路由问题，把目标查询解码解耦为相机、激光雷达和融合三条并行分支，并借助场景可靠性先验与目标级证据进行查询级路由，在 nuScenes 测试集取得 72.5 mAP / 74.4 NDS，并在传感器损坏与环境变化下展现鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机-激光雷达融合 3D 检测器通常基于紧耦合多模态表征解码目标查询，形成强模态间依赖；在驾驶条件受损时，不可靠模态会干扰可靠模态的独立证据，导致预测次优。同时，模态可靠性在全局场景与单个目标查询层面均会变化，需要更细粒度的自适应融合决策。

Method: 将鲁棒相机-激光雷达融合重新表述为场景感知分支路由问题，提出 SARFusion。该方法不依赖单一融合表征产生检测结果，而是将目标查询解码解耦为三条并行推理分支：相机分支、激光雷达分支、相机-激光雷达融合分支。以从全局驾驶上下文估计的场景可靠性先验为引导，并结合目标级证据，将每个查询路由到最合适的分支，实现查询级路由。

Result: 在 nuScenes 测试集上达到 72.5 mAP 与 74.4 NDS；大量分析表明其在传感器损坏和环境变化等挑战性条件下具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 查询级分支路由可缓解有害的跨模态干扰，同时在互补线索可信时保留多模态融合收益，为鲁棒 3D 目标检测提供有效范式。

Abstract: Camera-LiDAR fusion has become a prevailing paradigm for 3D object detection in autonomous driving. However, existing fusion detectors often establish strong inter-modality dependencies by decoding object queries from tightly coupled multimodal representations. Under corrupted driving conditions, such dependencies make the detector vulnerable to unreliable modalities, where degraded observations may interfere with reliable modality-specific evidence and lead to suboptimal predictions. Moreover, modality reliability can vary across both global driving scenes and individual object queries, requiring adaptive fusion decisions at a finer granularity. To bridge this gap, we reformulate robust camera-LiDAR fusion as a scene-aware branch routing problem and propose SARFusion, a robust 3D object detector. Instead of producing detections from a single fused representation, SARFusion decouples object-query decoding into three parallel reasoning branches: a camera branch, a LiDAR branch, and a camera-LiDAR fusion branch. Guided by a Scene Reliability Prior estimated from the global driving context, SARFusion further incorporates object-level evidence to route each query to the most suitable branch. This query-wise routing strategy alleviates harmful cross-modal interference while preserving the benefits of multimodal fusion when complementary cues are trustworthy. On the nuScenes test set, SARFusion achieves strong performance with 72.5 mAP and 74.4 NDS. Extensive analyses demonstrate its robustness under challenging conditions, including sensor corruptions and environmental changes.

</details>


### [17] [TOLA: Text-aware One-Step Latent Adaptation for Diffusion-based Text Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29240)
*Yike Xu,Yue Shi,Yong Guo,Jiezhang Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TOLA：一种无需迭代图像-文本扩散的文本感知单步潜空间适配框架，用于文本图像超分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的文本图像超分辨率（TSR）方法存在两大问题：一是需要多步预测高分辨率图像或文本先验，计算开销与推理延迟极高；二是错误的文本先验会被反复注入去噪过程，使图像与文本预测相互强化，将早期识别错误逐步放大为清晰但语义错误的字符。

Method: 提出TOLA框架，包含两个核心模块：（1）置信度加权文本条件模块，仅构建一次语义条件，在不可靠OCR预测污染图像重建前对其进行抑制；（2）轻量级潜空间残差校正模块，显式估计并校正结构化残差误差，以恢复缺失或畸变的笔画细节。整体避免迭代式图像-文本扩散。

Result: 在CTR-TSR-Test（×4）与RealCE-200两个基准上，所有评价指标均达到当前最优性能；在CTR-TSR-Test上PSNR较现有基于扩散的TSR方法至少高出2.72 dB。

Conclusion: TOLA通过单步潜空间适配与置信度加权文本条件，规避了多步扩散的高成本与误差累积问题，在保证可读性的同时显著提升重建质量与效率。

Abstract: Text image super-resolution (TSR) aims to recover visually faithful and readable text under unknown degradations. Existing diffusion-based methods typically rely on multi-step prediction of either the high-resolution image or its text prior, resulting in prohibitive computational cost and inference latency. More critically, an erroneous text prior may be repeatedly injected into the denoising process, causing image and text predictions to reinforce each other and progressively amplify an early recognition error into a sharp yet semantically incorrect character. To address these limitations, we propose TOLA, a Text-aware One-step Latent Adaptation framework without iterative image-text diffusion. TOLA consists of two key modules. First, a confidence-weighted text conditioning module constructs the semantic condition only once and suppresses unreliable OCR predictions before they contaminate image reconstruction. Second, a lightweight latent residual correction module explicitly estimates and corrects the structured residual errors to recover missing or distorted stroke details. Extensive experiments demonstrate our state-of-the-art performance across all evaluation metrics on both CTR-TSR-Test ($\times 4$) and RealCE-200 benchmarks. It is worth noting that our TOLA consistently surpasses existing diffusion-based TSR methods by at least 2.72 dB in PSNR on CTR-TSR-Test.

</details>


### [18] [Deep learning of longitudinal visual fields predicts glaucoma progression rate and identifies fast progressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29256)
*Taiabur Rahman,Siddiqur Rahman,Muhammad Moniruzzaman,Ummay Kawsar,Sayedatunnessa Ratna,Shadman Siddique,Rafsan Siddique,Tausif Ahmad,Tahsin Ahmad,Golam Rabbani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GLAM：一种基于注意力融合与偶然不确定性、输入纵向 Humphrey 24-2 总偏差序列及五项临床特征的深度学习框架，可预测青光眼 MD 与 VFI 进展速率，仅用视野数据即可达到与多模态流程相当的预后性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 青光眼是不可逆致盲的首要原因，及时识别快速进展者对防止残疾至关重要。但现有临床做法基于 MD 对时间做普通最小二乘回归估计进展速率，需 6–10 次视野检查、历时数年才能获得可靠斜率，时效性差且负担重，因此需要更高效、准确的进展预测方法。

Method: 提出 GLAM（Glaucoma Longitudinal Analysis Model）深度学习框架：输入纵向 Humphrey 24-2 总偏差（TD）序列，并结合五项临床特征，通过基于注意力的融合机制（attention-based fusion）以及偶然不确定性（aleatoric uncertainty）建模，直接预测 MD 进展速率与视野指数（VFI）进展速率。

Result: 在公开的 University of Washington Humphrey Visual Field 数据集（4,276 只患者眼）上，GLAM 的 MD 进展速率平均绝对误差为 0.139 dB yr⁻¹（R² = 0.927，相较 ridge 基线降低 73.5%）；快速进展者检测 AUC 达 0.990。

Conclusion: 仅使用常规采集的视野（perimetry）数据的深度学习，即可在进展预后方面匹配多模态流程，说明 VF-only 方法具备与多模态方案相当的预测能力，有望简化青光眼进展评估流程。

Abstract: Glaucoma is the leading cause of irreversible blindness, and timely identification of fast progressors is essential to prevent disability. Current practice estimates progression by ordinary least-squares regression of mean deviation (MD) on time, requiring 6--10 visual field (VF) tests over several years to obtain a reliable slope. We present GLAM (Glaucoma Longitudinal Analysis Model), a deep learning framework that ingests longitudinal Humphrey 24-2 total deviation sequences with five clinical features and predicts MD and visual field index progression rates using attention-based fusion and aleatoric uncertainty. On the open-access University of Washington Humphrey Visual Field dataset (4,276 patient-eyes), GLAM achieved an MD-rate mean absolute error of 0.139 dB yr$^{-1}$ ($R^2 = 0.927$; 73.5% reduction over a ridge baseline) and an AUC of 0.990 for fast-progressor detection. VF-only deep learning can match multimodal pipelines for progression prognostication using routinely collected perimetry alone.

</details>


### [19] [Hyperbolic Multimodal Continual Learning: A Closest-Admissible Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29329)
*Jiahong Liu,Ming Shen,Xiaohao Liu,Rex Ying,Menglin Yang,Tat-Seng Chua,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双曲多模态持续学习框架 HMCL：通过将所有模态限制到同一共享双曲等距变换，显式保持洛伦兹几何，并以最近可容许（CA/MR）校正约束 AdamW 更新，缓解顺序学习中的关系扭曲与灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法主要保护参数、回放样本或欧氏特征子空间，但用于双曲多模态模型时未显式保持洛伦兹几何。该几何联合编码模态内相似性、跨模态对应与语义层级，顺序更新可能在维持任务分数的情况下仍扭曲已学关系。

Method: 作者证明保持旧多模态几何等价于让所有模态共享同一双曲等距变换，并由此诱导一族可容许的一阶参数变化。提出联合最近可容许（CA）校正，保留最匹配候选模态更新的共享旋转；最小旋转（MR）特例将该旋转固定为零。两种变体均校正 AdamW 实现的参数位移，并通过任务锚定限制任务内累积，同时保留学习自由度。

Result: 在包含 16 个任务的统一分类-检索流及三个双曲骨干上，HMCL 相比顺序微调和四个持续学习基线提升最终性能与反向迁移；HMCL-CA 在所有骨干上取得最高 Overall 分数。模态扩展流验证了检索增益。表征分析显示径向、角度、跨模态及成对距离漂移减少 81.2%–95.5%；ImageNet-WordNet 上语义祖先与径向层级表现更好。

Conclusion: HMCL 通过共享双曲等距与可容许更新校正，显式维护洛伦兹几何，在双曲多模态持续学习中兼顾稳定性与可塑性，并改善性能、反向迁移及语义层级保持。

Abstract: Existing continual-learning methods protect parameters, replayed examples, or Euclidean feature subspaces. When applied to hyperbolic multimodal models, they do not explicitly preserve the Lorentz geometry that jointly encodes within-modality similarity, cross-modal correspondence, and semantic hierarchy; sequential updates can therefore retain task scores while still distorting previously learned relations. We address this gap with Hyperbolic Multimodal Continual Learning (HMCL). We show that preserving the old multimodal geometry amounts to restricting all modalities to one shared hyperbolic isometry, which induces a family of admissible first-order parameter changes. We formulate a joint closest-admissible (CA) correction that retains the shared rotation best matching the candidate modal updates; its minimal-rotation (MR) special case fixes this rotation to zero. Both variants correct the displacement realized by AdamW, and task anchoring bounds within-task accumulation while preserving learning freedom. Across a unified 16-task classification-retrieval stream with three hyperbolic backbones, HMCL improves final performance and backward transfer over sequential fine-tuning and four continual-learning baselines; HMCL-CA gives the highest Overall score on every backbone. A modality-extended stream confirms the retrieval gains. Representation analyses find 81.2 to 95.5 percent less radial, angular, cross-modal, and paired-distance drift; ImageNet-WordNet results show better semantic ancestry and radial hierarchy.

</details>

### [20] [RAPID: Robot Agentic Programming from Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30249)
*Yuyao Liu,Jiayuan Mao,David Hsu,Leslie Pack Kaelbling,Tomás Lozano-Pérez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAPID 提出一种"机器人智能体式编程"框架：仅凭单次人类视觉演示，即可自动生成、验证并迭代精化机器人程序，实现跨物体位姿、形状、材质与环境的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体（coding agents）在复杂编程任务上已取得巨大成功，但如何将其能力迁移到机器人系统尚缺乏有效途径。传统从演示学习（LfD）往往难以生成可验证、可复用、可跨场景迁移的程序，因此需要一种能将演示自动转化为可测试、可执行、可迭代改进的机器人程序的方法。

Method: 给定单次视觉人类演示，RAPID 自动推断代码精化闭环所需的三项要素：(i) 可测试的任务规范；(ii) 用于机器人执行的动作原语；(iii) 支持程序执行与验证的交互式环境。为提升程序在演示场景之外的可复用性，采用"物体中心的关系式程序表示"：将动作原语表达为轨迹优化程序以实现物体级运动效果，并通过关系约束在运行时组合，从而捕获场景特定几何关系，关注演示策略的底层结构而非具体运动轨迹。

Result: 在仿真中对八项具挑战性的接触丰富非抓取（nonprehensile）操作任务以及 LIBERO-Pro 基准中的通用抓取（prehensile）操作任务进行评估；同时在真实 Franka 机械臂上完成部署，并对全部八项非抓取任务进行评测。所有实验中 RAPID 均表现强劲，并在物体位姿、形状、材质和环境方面展现出良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: RAPID 验证了借助编码智能体的"自动生成—验证—精化"循环，可从单次演示中构建可复用、可泛化的机器人程序；配合物体中心的关系式表示与轨迹优化原语，方法在仿真与真机上均取得有效结果，为机器人程序的自动编程提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Coding agents have demonstrated enormous success in solving complex programming problems. To leverage their potential for robot systems, this work introduces Robot Agentic Programming from Demonstrations (RAPID), which automatically generates, verifies, and refines robot programs, given a single visual human demonstration. The iterative agentic loop of code refinement requires several key ingredients: (i) a testable task specification, (ii) action primitives for robot execution, and (iii) an interactive environment for program execution and verification. RAPID infers all three from the demonstration automatically. To make the resulting program reusable beyond the demonstration setting, RAPID uses an object-centric relational program representation that focuses on the underlying structure of the demonstrated strategy rather than the specific motion per se: it expresses the action primitives as trajectory-optimization programs that realize object-level motion effects, while composing them through relational constraints that capture scene-specific geometry at run time. We evaluated RAPID in simulation on eight challenging contact-rich nonprehensile manipulation tasks as well as general prehensile manipulation tasks in the LIBERO-Pro benchmark. We also successfully deployed it on a real Franka arm and evaluated on all eight nonprehensile tasks. In all experiments, RAPID demonstrated strong performance, with generalization over object pose, shape, material, and environment. Website: https://yuyaoliu.me/projects/rapid.

</details>


### [21] [Representation World Model: Learning States, Transition and Executable Plans in Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29171)
*Yijun Yuan,Weicheng Zheng,Weibang Wang,Minghui Qin,Chang Sun,Junhao Huang,Kenan Li,Anmin Liu,Yicheng Yao,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出表示世界模型（RWM），直接在表示空间学习状态、转移与可执行计划，将规划融入表示几何，无需递归推演或动作空间搜索；在连续控制与机器人操作任务上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型通常将隐表示与显式动力学模型联合学习，并依赖搜索、优化或基于策略的预测进行规划，存在递归展开与动作空间搜索的开销。本文希望把规划直接嵌入学习到的表示空间中。

Method: RWM通过从端点表示构建潜在路径，并沿路径施加局部逆动力学监督来学习表示几何，使路径保留任务相关的状态与转移信息。推理时，在当前表示与目标表示之间直接构造潜在路径，再用逆动力学恢复对应动作，不进行递归 rollout 或动作空间搜索。

Result: 在连续控制基准上，RWM实现了有效的直接规划；在机器人操作任务上，结果进一步表明其可扩展到更复杂的具身控制任务。

Conclusion: 直接在表示空间中进行规划，是传统世界模型规划范式的一个有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: We propose the Representation World Model (RWM), which learns states, transitions, and executable plans directly in representation space. Unlike existing world models that typically learn latent representations together with explicit dynamics models and perform planning through search, optimization, or policy-based prediction, RWM directly incorporates planning into the learned representation geometry. RWM learns the representation geometry by applying inverse-dynamics supervision locally along latent paths constructed from endpoint representations, requiring these paths to preserve task-relevant state and transition information. At inference, planning is performed by directly constructing a latent path between the current and goal representations, with inverse dynamics used to recover the corresponding actions, without recursive rollouts or action-space search. Experiments on continuous-control benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of RWM for direct planning, while results on robotic manipulation further show its potential to extend to more complex embodied control tasks. These results suggest that planning directly in representation space provides a promising alternative to conventional world-model planning.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [22] [When Should Forecasting Agents Reason? Behavioral Stress Tests for Reliability Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28475)
*Yufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文研究二元预测任务中语言模型代理应何时检索、推理、参考市场先验或使用历史类比，提出 ReliabilityRoute 结构性干预，按可靠性特征路由行为；核心发现是机制选择依赖证据来源，更多推理并非总是更好，且自适应路由在可审计约束下略有增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测代理日益组合语言模型推理、检索、集成与校准，但何时应信任每种行为仍不清晰。作者将检索、推理、参考市场先验、使用历史类比视为可观测的代理行为，而非隐藏实现细节，以检验不同证据来源下机制选择的可靠性。

Method: 在 ForecastBench 风格二元预测任务上，引入 ReliabilityRoute 结构性干预，使用历史覆盖率、市场先验可用性、来源先验锐度、证据强度、证据分歧和时间跨度等可靠性特征来引导代理行为。方法包括 2024 年拟合的固定规则，以及根据已解决历史批次重拟合阈值的 walk-forward 自适应规则，并与手工分类法及确定性系统比较。

Result: 固定规则在不硬编码来源名称决策的情况下接近手工分类法；walk-forward 自适应规则在后续 16 个 LLM 版本上取得确定性系统中最佳平均 Brier 分数。但增益有限，历史与搜索基线仍极具竞争力。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是行为压力测试：更多推理并不总是更优；预测代理应首先估计哪个证据来源值得控制，路由策略本身应在可审计约束下自适应，并提供可复现工件。

Abstract: Forecasting agents increasingly combine language-model reasoning, retrieval, ensembling, and calibration, but it remains unclear when each behavior should be trusted. We study this question on ForecastBench-style binary forecasting tasks, treating the choice to retrieve, reason, defer to a market prior, or use a historical analog as an observable agent behavior rather than a hidden implementation detail. Our central finding is that mechanism choice is source-dependent: structured analogs dominate for some data-generating processes, while market/crowd-style and conservative baselines are better for others. We introduce ReliabilityRoute, a structural intervention that steers forecasting-agent behavior using reliability features such as historical coverage, market-prior availability, source-prior sharpness, evidence strength, evidence disagreement, and horizon. A fixed 2024-fitted rule closely matches a hand taxonomy without hard-coded source-name decisions, while a walk-forward self-adjusting rule refits thresholds from previously resolved vintages and obtains the best mean Brier score among our deterministic systems across 16 later LLM vintages. The gain is modest and historical/search baselines remain highly competitive. The main contribution is therefore a behavioral stress test showing that more reasoning is not always better; forecasting agents should first estimate which evidence source deserves control, routing policies should themselves adapt under auditable constraints, and reproducibility artifacts are available at https://github.com/louiswang524/forcastagent

</details>


### [23] [TW3Cast: A Frozen Router of Lightly Fine-Tuned Foundation Models for Time-Series Forecasting on GIFT-Eval, Selected Entirely on the Training Split](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28506)
*Nathan Thierry,Andre-Louis Rochet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TW3Cast 是一个不含智能体、不调用语言模型的时间序列预测系统，在 GIFT-Eval 上以平均 MASE 排名第 3（130 个条目，截至 2026-09-14）。其核心是一次性在训练集上计算并冻结的路由表：对 97 个数据集/频率/预测步长配置，分别选择专家、分位数混合、基模型混合或回测锦标赛。专家为轻量微调的公开基础模型 Chronos-2、TiRex、Toto。全路由平均 MASE 排名 19.4，显著优于最佳基模型单独使用（33.8）和全配置锦标赛（38.0）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强排行榜方法多依赖智能体或语言模型进行推理、生成或选择预测，成本高且复杂。本文希望验证：无需智能体与语言模型，仅靠一次性冻结的路由表、轻量微调的公开基础模型和严格的选择机制，是否也能在 GIFT-Eval 等基准上取得高排名，并提供可复现、低成本的预测方案。

Method: 系统对 97 个 dataset-frequency-horizon 配置维护一张训练集上计算后冻结的选择表，每个配置可选四种模式：1）专家：Chronos-2、TiRex 或 Toto 的 LoRA 或全量微调，训练数据经显式规则清洗与增强；2）包含专家的分位数混合；3）基模型混合；4）在训练集切出的 backtest 上进行选择锦标赛。所有决策均在 backtest 上完成。专家一旦在 backtest 上击败锦标赛即被接纳，候选成本仅为数 MB 与数分钟 GPU，失败不影响现有选择。采用三重防护机制控制选择偏差：精度与校准双重准则、针对训练中见过该序列的候选的非对称边际、保守的逐窗口门控。选择规则本身通过时间元回测确定。

Result: 在 GIFT-Eval 上，最佳基模型单独使用平均 MASE 排名为 33.8；锦标赛在所有配置上使用为 38.0；完整路由器达到 19.4。TW3Cast 以平均 MASE 排名位列 130 个条目第 3，仅次于排行榜 agentic 类别中的两个多步系统。系统不运行智能体或语言模型，选择表冻结，专家为轻量微调的公开基础模型。

Conclusion: TW3Cast 表明，通过冻结路由表、轻量专家微调、backtest 驱动的准入与多重偏差防护，可在不依赖智能体或语言模型的情况下取得强竞争力。作者释放路由表、专家索引、固定基模型版本、提交分数文件及公开分数日期快照，并提供一个脚本复现论文中的全部排行榜数字，强调可复现性与低成本候选评估。

Abstract: TW3Cast is a time-series forecasting system that reaches position 3 of 130 entries on the GIFT-Eval benchmark by mean MASE rank, as of 2026-09-14. The two entries above it belong to the leaderboard's agentic category, multi-step systems that use agents or language models to reason about, generate or select forecasts. TW3Cast runs no agent and no language model. Its selection is a table computed once on the training split and then frozen, and its experts are public foundation models lightly fine-tuned on those training splits. For each of the 97 dataset, frequency and horizon configurations, the table serves one of four modes: a specialist, which is a LoRA or full fine-tune of Chronos-2, TiRex or Toto whose training data was cleaned and enriched by explicit rules; a quantile blend that contains a specialist; a blend of base models; or a selection tournament played on a backtest carved from the training split. Every decision in the table was taken on that backtest. A specialist is admitted the moment it beats the tournament there, so a candidate costs a few megabytes and minutes of GPU time, and a failed candidate changes nothing. Three guarded mechanisms protect the selection from its own biases: a dual accuracy and calibration criterion, an asymmetric margin against candidates that saw the series during training, and conservative per-window gates. The selection rules themselves were chosen inside a temporal meta-backtest. The best base model served alone reaches a mean MASE rank of 33.8, the tournament served on every configuration reaches 38.0, and the full router reaches 19.4. The routing table, the expert index, the pinned base-model revisions, the submitted score file and the dated snapshot of the public scores are released, and every leaderboard number in this paper regenerates from them by one script.

</details>


### [24] [PAWS: Policy-driven Agentic World Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28547)
*Tiviatis Sim,Jia Hui Woon,Xinming Gao,Chen Gao,Fengbin Zhu,Zheng Huanhuan,Chua Tat Seng,Kenji Kawaguchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PAWS 是一个政策驱动的智能体世界仿真数据集，整合 36 个美国金融政策事件、1.2 万余条新闻与 6.5 万余条利益相关者行动，并与市场收益对齐以支持历史锚定的多智能体仿真回放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政策干预会通过公共传播、机构决策与利益相关者响应进行传导，但现有金融多智能体仿真数据集很少将这些过程与时间对齐的历史证据相连接，缺乏可用于仿真回放的历史锚定数据。

Method: 构建 PAWS 数据集：覆盖 36 个经核实的美国金融与经济政策事件、12,727 条政策相关新闻、65,291 条有来源支撑的利益相关者行动。每条行动关联其支撑新闻，并用多层事件框架表示（交互模式、金融行动族与子类型、语义属性、对外部分类体系的条件映射）；实体归一化为标准组织；行动与每日市场收益上下文对齐以支持回放。通过 2,522 条分层抽样，由独立 AI 与人类评审进行一致性验证。

Result: 在 2,522 条分层行动样本上，独立 AI 与人类评审在交互模式上初始一致率达 89.4%，分歧经裁定解决。对 2008 年卖空禁令与 2001 年十进制化的案例研究，在密集与稀疏新闻两种设定下均复原了有据可查的政策时间线与相关市场模式。回放研究表明，高准确率可能掩盖对稀有利益相关者行动的漏检。

Conclusion: PAWS 为历史锚定的金融多智能体仿真提供了可审计的数据基座，可用于评估智能体影响力、政策响应级联以及"行动—结果"对齐；同时指出行动时机识别与校准是后续研究的核心挑战。

Abstract: Policy interventions propagate through public communication, institutional decisions, and stakeholder responses, yet datasets for financial multi-agent simulation rarely connect these processes to temporally aligned historical evidence. We introduce PAWS, a Policy-driven Agentic World Simulation dataset covering 36 verified U.S. financial and economic policy episodes, 12,727 policy-linked news records, and 65,291 source-grounded stakeholder actions. Each action is linked to its supporting news and represented by a multi-layer event frame capturing its interaction mode, financial-action family and subtype, semantic attributes, and conditional mappings to external taxonomies. Entities are resolved to normalized organizations, and actions are aligned with daily market-return context to support policy-agent simulation replay. On 2,522 stratified action samples, independent AI and human reviewers achieved 89.4% initial agreement on interaction mode, with disagreements subsequently adjudicated. Case studies of the 2008 short-selling ban and 2001 decimalization recover documented policy timelines and associated market patterns across both dense and sparse news settings. A replay study further shows that high accuracy can mask failure to detect rare stakeholder actions, identifying action timing and calibration as central challenges. PAWS provides an auditable substrate for evaluating agent influence, policy-response cascades, and action-outcome alignment in historically grounded financial simulations.

</details>


### [25] [Pistis Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28554)
*Heyun Chen,Xiaohan Lan,Jiaxi Li,Zhilin Lu,Qi She,Weiwen Xu,Fei Yu,Yujie Zhong,Jinghuan Chen,Zijian Feng,Siyu Jiao,Yiheng Lin,Xinhao Wang,Sihan Yang,Jieyu You,Changbin Zhang,Hengyu Zhang,Xudong Zhang,Yunqing Zhao,Shuai Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Pistis 多模态大模型家族（27B/9B，基于 Qwen3.6/Qwen3.5），通过通用可扩展后训练框架构建，核心为交错蒸馏与强化学习（IDRL），并衍生 Thinking 与 Agentic 变体，同时提出系统级 Pistis-Auto-Harnessing（PAH）优化推理执行框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练中，蒸馏与强化学习通常被单独优化或通过静态联合损失组合，难以兼顾知识迁移、优化稳定性与长程智能体轨迹中的信用分配，且易产生能力权衡；同时模型参数之外的推理执行框架性能提升尚未被充分利用。

Method: 1）先进行大规模多模态监督微调（SFT）建立基础能力；2）提出 IDRL：在同一训练循环内交替执行 on-policy 蒸馏与强化学习，而非孤立优化或静态联合损失；3）在两个参数规模上训练 Pistis-Thinking（强化多模态推理）与 Pistis-Agentic（加入智能体轨迹数据，支持长程规划、迭代推理和工具使用）；4）提出 PAH，通过迭代优化自动改进智能体推理执行框架，不更新模型参数、不增加交互预算。

Result: Pistis-Agentic 在多模态搜索方面表现突出；两个参数规模的模型均优于对应基座模型；IDRL 带来更有效的知识迁移、更高优化稳定性、更精确的长程智能体轨迹信用分配，并缓解常见能力权衡；PAH 在不更新参数和增加交互预算的情况下提升模型表现。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，交替式蒸馏-强化学习后训练与推理执行框架自动优化可协同提升多模态大模型及智能体能力，为构建可扩展、多规格、面向推理与智能体任务的多模态模型提供通用后训练范式。

Abstract: We introduce the Pistis model family, comprising 27B- and 9B-parameter multimodal large language models built on Qwen3.6 and Qwen3.5, respectively, and developed through a general and scalable post-training framework. The framework first establishes a strong foundation through large-scale multimodal supervised fine-tuning (SFT). Building on this SFT foundation, we propose Interleaved Distillation and Reinforcement Learning (IDRL), a novel post-training paradigm that tightly integrates on-policy distillation and reinforcement learning within a single training loop. By alternating between the two objectives, rather than optimizing either in isolation or combining them in a static joint loss, IDRL enables more effective knowledge transfer, greater optimization stability, and more precise credit assignment for long-horizon agentic trajectories, leading to stronger performance while mitigating common capability trade-offs. At both model scales, the framework produces two specialized variants: Pistis-Thinking, designed to strengthen deep multimodal reasoning, and Pistis-Agentic, which additionally incorporates agentic trajectory data to support long-horizon planning, iterative reasoning, and tool use. Pistis-Agentic is particularly strong in multimodal search. Both scales outperform their corresponding base models. Beyond model-parameter optimization, we further introduce Pistis-Auto-Harnessing (PAH), a system-level method that automatically improves the agent's inference harness through iterative optimization. Experiments demonstrate that PAH enhances the model performance without updating the model parameters or increasing the interaction budget.

</details>


### [26] [BaseCamp --- An Agentic AI Framework for Automating DNA Sequencing Data Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28557)
*Eranga Bandara,Xueping Liang,Asanga Gunaratna,Tharaka Hewa,Abdul Rahman,Peter Foytik,Safdar H. Bouk,Sachini Rajapakse,Isurunima Kularathna,Pramoda Karunarathna,Chalani Rajapakse,Ng Wee Keong,Kasun De Zoysa,Amin Hass,Wathsala Herath,Ross Gore,Ravi Mukkamala,Nihal Siriwardanagea,Gihan Siriwardanagea,Aruna Withanage,Nilaan Loganathan,Sachin Shetty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BaseCamp 是一个面向 DNA 测序流程“决策层”的智能体 AI 框架，将流程分解为六个专用 AI 代理，用本地部署、领域微调的 LLM 联盟进行判断与协调，而实际比对、变异检测和注释仍由既有生物信息学工具执行。评估显示其配置与专家实践一致，过滤账本可追溯，跨阶段异常检测能发现常规执行监控遗漏的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNA 测序流程的执行层（质控、比对、变异检测、注释）已可由工作流管理系统可靠编排，但围绕执行的决策层仍依赖人工：阈值选择、边界变异判读、异常诊断、专家复核判定等。这些决策重复、依赖经验、操作者间不一致且常缺乏记录，因此需要自动化该决策层。

Method: 提出 BaseCamp 智能体 AI 框架，将测序流程分解为六个专用 AI 代理：样本接收与质控、比对、变异检测、注释、跨阶段监测、报告。关键设计是代理不直接执行序列分析，而由成熟工具完成比对、检测与注释；代理负责工具选择、配置、输出解释与后续决策，从而将语言模型推理限制在更可靠的判断层，并保留既有工具的可重复性。推理由经微调的领域专用 LLM 联盟提供，由中央推理 LLM 协调，本地执行以确保测序数据不出运行环境，并采用 human-in-the-loop 编排。

Result: 评估表明：代理生成的配置与专家实践一致；显式过滤账本使原本无痕移除的过滤过程可检查；跨阶段异常检测可发现执行监控遗漏的情况。

Conclusion: BaseCamp 为科学数据流程的智能体自动化提供了可推广蓝图，其核心价值在于自动化测序流程的决策层，而非替代既有分析工具。

Abstract: DNA sequencing pipelines, spanning quality control, alignment, variant calling, and annotation, are now reliably executed by workflow management systems that orchestrate established bioinformatics tools at scale. What remains manual is the decision layer surrounding that execution: selecting quality thresholds appropriate to a sample and platform, adjudicating borderline variant calls, diagnosing anomalies, and determining which findings warrant expert review. These decisions are repetitive, judgment-intensive, inconsistent across operators, and frequently undocumented. This paper introduces BaseCamp, a novel agentic AI framework for automating the decision layer of DNA sequencing pipelines. The framework decomposes the pipeline into six specialized AI agents, covering sample intake and quality control, alignment, variant calling, annotation, cross-stage monitoring, and reporting. Critically, BaseCamp agents do not perform sequence analysis: established tools execute alignment, calling, and annotation, while the agents select among them, configure them, interpret their output, and decide what follows. This confines language model reasoning to the judgment layer where it is reliable and preserves the reproducibility existing tooling guarantees. Agent reasoning is powered by a consortium of fine-tuned, domain-specialized large language models coordinated by a central reasoning LLM, executing locally so no sequencing data leaves the operating environment, under human-in-the-loop orchestration. Evaluation shows agent-generated configurations are concordant with expert practice, that an explicit filtering ledger renders inspectable what filtering otherwise removes without trace, and that cross-stage anomaly detection surfaces conditions execution monitoring misses. BaseCamp offers a generalizable blueprint for agentic automation of scientific data pipelines.

</details>


### [27] [DEEPO: Dual-Entropy Enhanced Policy Optimization for Hallucination in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28570)
*Yingxuan Zhuang,Miao Pan,Wangjie Gan,Jingxiao Yang,Fan Wang,Weiming Liu,Cheng Tan,Xuhong Zhang,Jintao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DEEPO 从 rollout 与优化两级修复 RL 修正链路：用语义熵触发的专家前缀恢复困难查询的优势方差，用优势符号感知的 Renyi 预处理穿透 logit 饱和，从而在保持准确率与稳定性的同时降低 MLLM 幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习被广泛用于提升多模态大模型（MLLM）的推理能力，但其对幻觉的抑制效果并不稳定。作者将其归因于"从奖励到参数更新"的修正链路上的两个薄弱点：(1) rollout 层面，高语义熵的困难查询常产生"一致错误"的样本组，使组相对优势（group-relative advantage）坍缩为零，而这恰恰是幻觉风险最高的地方；(2) 优化层面，高置信但错误的 token 在梯度上"不可见"——类别策略的期望 score-gradient 范数随分布变尖而消失，导致最需纠正的预测获得最弱的更新。

Method: 提出 Dual-Entropy Enhanced Policy Optimization（DEEPO），双阶段增强：(1) 信号方差正则化——在语义熵触发的高不确定查询上注入 expert prefix（专家前缀），提供有依据的续写作为直接监督，恢复组内优势方差；(2) 梯度预处理——采用优势符号感知的 Renyi 预处理，抵消 logit 层饱和，使修正能到达"高置信但错误"的 token。

Result: 两条分支均单独优于 GRPO；二者在评测套件中复杂度最高、最长程的任务 VideoMMMU 上交互作用统计显著（+4.0，95% CI [1.1, 6.9]），在其他任务上呈可加性。DEEPO 在降低幻觉的同时保持了准确率与训练稳定性。

Conclusion: DEEPO 通过在 rollout 与优化两个层面同时增强"奖励→参数更新"的修正链路，能够在保持准确率与训练稳定性的前提下降低多模态大模型的幻觉；其双分支各自独立有效，且在最长程任务上呈现显著交互增益。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is widely used to sharpen reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs), yet its effect on hallucination is uneven. We trace this to two weak points in the \emph{correction chain} from reward to parameter update. At the rollout level, hard queries---those with high semantic entropy---frequently produce unanimously wrong sample groups, collapsing the group-relative
  advantage to zero exactly where hallucination risk is highest. At the optimization level, confident-but-wrong tokens are gradient-invisible: a categorical policy's expected score-gradient norm vanishes as its distribution sharpens, so the predictions that most need correction receive the weakest updates. We propose Dual-Entropy Enhanced Policy Optimization (DEEPO), a dual-stage enhancement combining signal
  variance regularization with gradient preconditioning: semantic-entropy-triggered expert prefixes inject grounded continuations on high-uncertainty queries, providing direct supervision and restoring advantage variance, while advantage-sign-aware Renyi preconditioning counteracts logit-level saturation so correction reaches confident errors in the operational confidence regime. Both branches improve over GRPO individually; their interaction is statistically significant on VideoMMMU---the most complex long-horizon task in our evaluation suite (+4.0$, 95\% CI [1.1, 6.9])---and additive elsewhere. DEEPO reduces hallucination while preserving accuracy and training stability.

</details>


### [28] [TWIST: A Proposed Benchmark for Intervention Quality in Conversational Memory, with a Human-Validated Draft-Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28575)
*Subrat Panda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TWIST 是一个面向长期对话记忆系统的基准套件，评测"干预质量"：系统能否在信念变化点正确检测、拦截、更新与受控回忆，并通过配对的反过度干预指标防止"一律报警"式作弊。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长对话记忆基准主要考察召回与提示式知识更新，近期工作也研究用户信念演化与记忆状态，但"干预质量"这一属性尚未被度量：即系统在其 ingest/recall/vet 接口中，是否能在信念变化点做出正确行为。

Method: TWIST 扩展 LoCoMo 的语料与评测框架，设四条赛道：无提示张力检测、用记录审查外发草稿、以当前信念作答并保留被取代历史、敏感回忆治理。每条 detect/block 指标都配一个"勿过度检测"对照，使用表面匹配的困难负例为误干预定价。基准自身先经独立、盲金标双标注与裁决、评判诱饵校准及可分性审计验证。

Result: 在人工验证的 Track B v1.0（161 项，裁决后 kappa=0.85）上，无任何配置能同时取得高矛盾召回、高困难负例特异度与高归因：flat-RAG 基线召回 0.76–0.97 真矛盾，但按后端不同误标 16–43% 表面匹配的安全草稿；一致性导向部署系统特异度 0.98–1.00，却只捕获 42% 真矛盾，形成纯召回分数看不到的权衡。13 配置基线阶梯定位原因：所有金标矛盾仅凭证据即可检出（recall 1.000），校准模型在给定完整转写时近乎解决该赛道，提示存在显著检索覆盖缺口；仅草稿下限则暴露模型相关风格先验。

Conclusion: TWIST 画像与召回分数并列，用于衡量记忆系统是否知道何时干预、何时不应干预，从而补足现有基准只测召回、无法刻画干预质量的空白。

Abstract: Long-conversation memory benchmarks increasingly test recall and prompted knowledge updates, and recent work studies evolving user beliefs and memory state. TWIST is a proposed benchmark suite for a complementary, unmeasured property: intervention quality -- whether a deployed memory system, exercised through its own ingest/recall/vet surface, acts correctly at belief change points. Four tracks cover unprompted tension detection, vetting outgoing drafts against the record, answering with current beliefs while preserving supersession history, and governing sensitive recall. The suite extends LoCoMo's corpora and harness, pairing every detect/block metric with a matched do-not-over-detect control: surface-matched hard negatives price false intervention, so no track can be gamed by flagging everything. The benchmark itself is validated first: independent, gold-blind double annotation with adjudication, judge decoy calibration, and a separability audit. On the human-validated Track B v1.0 key (161 items, post-adjudication kappa = 0.85), no tested configuration simultaneously achieves high contradiction recall, high hard-negative specificity, and high attribution: flat-RAG baselines detect 0.76-0.97 of true contradictions but falsely flag 16-43% of surface-matched safe drafts depending on backend, while a deployed coherence-oriented system almost never over-flags (0.98-1.00 specificity) yet catches 42% of true contradictions -- a trade-off no recall-only score can see. A 13-configuration baseline ladder localizes causes: every gold contradiction is detectable from its evidence alone (recall 1.000), calibrated models nearly solve the track given the full transcript -- consistent with substantial retrieval-coverage gaps -- and draft-only floors reveal model-dependent style priors. A system's TWIST profile, beside its recall score, measures whether memory knows when to intervene and when not to.

</details>


### [29] [Adversarial Closed-Loop Curriculum for Evolving Role-Playing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28609)
*Zheng Zhang,Liu Liu,Qi Chai,Deheng Ye,Peilin Zhao,Mao Zheng,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AdvRole：一种对抗式上下文改写框架，将角色扮演强化学习转变为闭环课程学习，通过Actor与Rewriter交替训练动态生成困难场景，在多个中英文及多语言基准上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的角色扮演智能体RL方法通常在训练开始前收集的固定场景池上进行训练，形成分布瓶颈：随着智能体能力提升，其表现较差的场景会发生变化，而训练分布却保持静态不变，导致训练场景无法持续针对智能体的薄弱区域。

Method: 提出AdvRole对抗式上下文改写框架，将角色扮演RL转化为闭环课程学习。框架在Actor（学习角色扮演）与Rewriter（将角色档案和对话上下文改写为针对Actor的困难场景）之间交替训练。Rewriter使用"性能差距奖励"进行训练，该奖励倾向于选择能相对原始场景降低当前Actor得分的改写，从而使场景池随Actor进化并持续聚焦于角色-上下文空间中尚未掌握的区域。

Result: 在涵盖英文和中文的三个角色扮演基准以及新发布的多语言基准上进行实验，AdvRole持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: AdvRole通过对抗式课程机制动态生成Actor特定困难场景，有效缓解了固定场景池的分布瓶颈问题，证明了闭环课程学习在角色扮演智能体训练中的有效性。

Abstract: Role-playing agents based on large language models have been widely applied in areas such as personalized assistance and social simulation. Recent RL methods typically train on a fixed scenario pool collected before learning begins. This creates a distributional bottleneck: as the agent improves, the scenarios where it performs poorly also change, while the training distribution remains static. Therefore, we propose AdvRole, an adversarial context rewriting framework that turns role-playing RL into a closed-loop curriculum. AdvRole alternates between an Actor that learns to role-play and a Rewriter that edits character profiles and dialogue contexts into actor-specific hard scenarios. The Rewriter is trained with a performance-gap reward, which favors rewrites that reduce the current Actor's score relative to the original scenario. As a result, the scenario pool evolves with the Actor and continuously targets under-mastered regions of the character-context space. Experiments on three role-playing benchmarks covering English and Chinese, as well as a new multilingual benchmark we release, show that AdvRole consistently outperforms baselines.

</details>


### [30] [Training Object Permanence in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28654)
*Haotian Zhang,Fengyuan Yu,Dezhi Luo,Haoran Sun,Zehong Zhao,Qingying Gao,Yihan Li,Siyuan An,Huayi Qin,Yilan Zhang,Zhengze Jiang,Pinyuan Feng,Renrui Zhang,Ziyu Guo,Letian Wang,Mengyue Yang,Kangfu Mei,Maijunxian Wang,Ran Ji,Vikash Kumar,Freda Shi,Chandra Sripada,Vincent C. Muller,Philip Torr,Alan Yuille,Nikolaus Kriegeskorte,Felix Juefei-Xu,Lvmin Zhang,Jieneng Chen,Yilun Du,Hokin Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 WROP：一个受认知科学启发的数据基础设施，含150个任务、6个认知类别、1.5M训练语料和300题评测；评估14个视频模型，16B世界模型PWM-WROP在续写模型中第一、总体第三，并开源数据、考试、权重及AWS Trainium2上的原生PyTorch训练栈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对象永久性与固体性是人类核心认知先验；近期视频生成模型作为世界模型展现出推理能力，但是否已涌现对象永久性尚不清楚。若未涌现，需用核心认知启发的数据集进行训练。

Method: 构建WROP数据基础设施：150个手工设计的认知科学任务，分6类；用Blender生成器在保持任务认知结构的同时随机化速度、光照、相机角度等干扰参数，每任务10,000+样本；发布1.5M训练语料和300题考试。评测3个reference-to-video、7个edit、4个continuation共14个视频模型，包括16B的PWM-WROP世界模型，并进行盲测成对Elo比较。

Result: 在盲测成对Elo研究中，PWM-WROP在continuation模型中排名第一，总体排名第三，仅次于两个reference-to-video模型之间的统计性并列。

Conclusion: 视频模型的对象永久性可通过认知科学启发的数据与评测体系进行系统检验和训练；作者开源数据、考试、模型答案、分数、权重，以及PWM——基于AWS Trainium2的原生PyTorch训练栈。

Abstract: Object permanence and solidity are hallmarks of human cognitive priors. Recent studies show that video generation models, a paradigmatic class of current world models, have begun to show emerged reasoning abilities, making them ideal candidates for building human-like physical intelligence. Do video models have emerged object permanence in them? If not, could we train them with a core-cognition inspired dataset? We introduce WROP (World Reasoning with Object Permanence), a data infrastructure of 150 hand-designed cognitive science inspired tasks, divided into six cognitive categories. We build Blender generators that randomize speed, lighting, camera angle, and other nuisance parameters while preserving each task's cognitive structure, yielding 10,000+ samples per task. We release a 1.5M-sample training corpus and a 300-question exam. On this exam we evaluate 14 video models: 3 reference-to-video, 7 edit, and 4 continuation, among which PWM-WROP, our 16B world model. In a blind pairwise Elo study, PWM-WROP ranks first among continuation models and third overall, behind only a statistical tie between two reference-to-video models. We release the data, exam, model answers, scores, weights, and PWM, our native-PyTorch training stack on AWS Trainium2.

</details>


### [31] [Beyond Surface Style: Aligning Multi-Turn User Simulators with Behavioral Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28690)
*Geng Chen,Ruotong Pan,Zhirui Yang,Qiqi He,Jiawei Chen,Zhang Yunfei,Chongyuan Chen,Minxuan Lv,Zheng Yang,Win-Bin Huang,Xiangyu Wu,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRACER 是一种两阶段训练的多轮用户模拟器，通过显式建模用户意图演化并结合层次化结果/轨迹奖励与偏差感知优势调制，使模拟行为对齐真实交互轨迹；TRACER-7B 在转化 F1、转化率误差和语义轨迹距离上优于基线，并通过图灵测试与动态营销基准验证其自然性与评估价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 忠实的用户模拟是大规模构建、评估和改进交互式 AI 的基础。然而，单个回复看似合理，并不保证模拟用户能复现真实交互中的意图演化与最终结果，因此需要让模拟行为与真实交互轨迹对齐。

Method: 提出 TRACER：一种显式建模用户演化意图的多轮用户模拟器，并学习使模拟行为与真实交互轨迹对齐。训练分两阶段：先在真实用户对话上做监督微调（SFT），再进行多轮强化学习（RL）。RL 阶段结合结果级与轨迹级的层次化奖励，并采用偏差感知优势调制（deviation-aware advantage modulation），以缓解长对话中的奖励稀疏与信用分配问题。基于真实客服会话构建参考队列进行评估，并进一步提出 Dynamic Marketing Benchmark。

Result: 在真实客服会话构建的参考队列上，TRACER-7B 相比最强基线提升 11.4 conversion F1，同时取得最低的组级转化率误差与语义轨迹距离，并能泛化到分布外场景。人类图灵测试中的识别准确率接近随机水平，支持生成对话的自然性。Dynamic Marketing Benchmark 通过模拟交互联合评估 LLM 的说服效果与回复质量，发现更高的回复质量并不必然对应更高的转化率。

Conclusion: TRACER 通过显式建模用户意图演化并对齐真实交互轨迹，提升了多轮用户模拟的忠实性；其衍生的 Dynamic Marketing Benchmark 进一步表明，回复质量高并不必然带来更高转化率。

Abstract: Faithful user simulation is fundamental to building, evaluating, and improving interactive AI at scale. However, plausible individual responses do not ensure that simulated users reproduce the intent evolution and outcomes observed in real interactions. We propose TRACER, a multi-turn user simulator that explicitly models users' evolving intent and learns to align simulated behavior with real interaction trajectories. TRACER is trained in two stages: supervised fine-tuning on real user dialogues, followed by multi-turn reinforcement learning. The RL stage combines hierarchical outcome- and trajectory-level rewards with deviation-aware advantage modulation, jointly mitigating reward sparsity and credit assignment in long dialogues. On real customer-service sessions organized into reference cohorts, TRACER-7B surpasses the strongest baseline by 11.4 conversion F1, while also achieving the lowest group-level conversion-rate error and semantic trajectory distance, and generalizing to out-of-distribution scenarios. Human Turing tests yield identification accuracy close to chance, supporting the perceived naturalness of generated conversations. Building on this simulator, we further introduce the Dynamic Marketing Benchmark, which jointly evaluates persuasion effectiveness and response quality of LLMs through simulated interactions, revealing that higher response quality does not necessarily correspond to higher conversion rates.

</details>


### [32] [Driving Epidemic Models with AI Agents: the Epydemix Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28692)
*Nicolò Gozzi,Ciro Cattuto,Alessandro Vespignani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Epydemix Agent Framework，在开源随机分室流行病建模库 Epydemix 之上增加智能体交互层，使大语言模型智能体可端到端完成建模任务，并通过 50 次智能体会话、5 类任务验证其在多数任务中减少交互轮次、输出 token 和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的 AI 智能体为科学软件提供了自然语言接口，但可靠性并非自动保证；需要一种可审计、可复现且易于智能体调用的框架，以降低直接使用 Python 接口时的交互开销与出错风险。

Method: 在 Epydemix 库上加设附加层，提供四项能力：可用模型与参数发现、声明式场景规范的预防性验证、通过已测试库代码执行、结果可检查性；每步读写独立输出包，支持从自然语言场景描述到定量结果、图表和解释的全流程建模。

Result: 通过一个比较新型呼吸道病毒疫苗接种策略的端到端案例展示工作流；并在 50 次智能体会话和 5 项建模任务中，将智能体使用该框架与直接使用 Python 接口对比，结果显示在多数任务上减少了交互轮次、输出 token 和成本，但在部分场景中以资源换取逐点可复现性。

Conclusion: 该框架能提升 AI 智能体与科学软件交互的可靠性和可审计性，并在多数建模任务中改善效率；其代价可能是在特定可复现性需求下增加资源消耗。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence agents based on large language models provide convenient natural language interfaces to scientific software, but reliability is not automatic. Here we introduce the Epydemix Agent Framework, an additive layer over Epydemix, an open-source Python library for stochastic compartmental epidemic modeling. The framework extends the library with four capabilities to facilitate interaction with an AI agent: discovery of available models and parameters, preventive validation of a declarative scenario specification, execution through tested library code, and inspectability of results. These capabilities let an agent handle the entire modeling process, from the natural-language description of the scenario to quantitative results, figures, and interpretation of findings without writing custom code. Each step reads input files and saves results in a separate output bundle, making the process auditable and reproducible. First, we show the end-to-end workflow with a case study comparing vaccination strategies for a novel respiratory virus. Second, we assessed the framework across 50 agent sessions and five modeling tasks by comparing the agent use of the framework against the direct use of the Python interface. The framework reduced turns, output tokens, and cost on most tasks, unless it trades resources for per-point reproducibility.

</details>


### [33] [Progressive Skill Discovery as Access Control for Tool-Using LLM Agents: Structural Governance through Role-Scoped Capability Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28693)
*Michael Stettler,Benjamin Girardet,Jonas Canton,Nicolas Corod*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: skilder 将能力封装为角色（roles），通过单一 MCP 服务器在技能学习范围内暴露工具，实现确定性的系统级授权与治理，同时允许智能体动态获取跨角色能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体接入庞大企业工具集时难以安全扩展：上下文窗口过大、工具选择质量下降、治理脆弱；仅靠提示词定义系统策略只是概率性建议而非硬约束。多智能体领域委派还会分散审计日志，且无法保证跨会话策略合规。

Method: 提出 skilder 框架：将能力打包为“角色”，每个角色包含技能、工具、指令及限制边界。智能体从最小角色目录开始，学习任务所需角色，并通过单一 MCP 服务器接收每个角色的技能、指令和工具。由于工具仅在学习到的技能内部到达智能体，同一服务器可确定性地执行已学习范围。评估对比 flat-context 工具选择与多智能体编排，覆盖 13 个任务、6 个模型，每个模型运行 10 次。

Result: 当模型完成发现流程并发起受治理调用时，skilder 的模拟授权层成功执行治理边界：未发生未授权工具调用或参数违规（如突破支出限制）。聚合任务通过率还反映模型是否遵循发现协议并满足响应质量检查；这些未通过并非授权失败。通过动态获取跨角色能力，skilder 在提供硬性系统级执法的同时保留了问题解决灵活性。

Conclusion: skilder 以角色化能力包与 MCP 服务器实现确定性授权和集中治理，可缓解大规模企业工具集下的上下文膨胀、工具选择退化与策略合规问题，并在安全约束下维持智能体的动态能力获取与任务灵活性。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents struggle to scale safely when exposed to vast enterprise toolsets. Providing an agent with access to every internal tool leads to oversized context windows, degraded tool selection, and severe governance vulnerabilities - as system policies defined purely in prompts remain probabilistic advice rather than hard constraints. Existing mitigations, such as multi-agent domain delegation, decentralize audit logs and fail to guarantee policy compliance across sessions.
